# Event · Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

Native dialogue order is preserved. Picture and movement commands without text are represented in the website.

## Story 410400101 · The Another World : Part 1

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

**Yui:** Mommy, Daddy! Good morning.

<!-- uid=410400101:1:1 -->

**Kirito:** Mornin', Yui.

<!-- uid=410400101:2:1 -->

**Asuna:** Good morning, Yui!

<!-- uid=410400101:3:1 -->

**Asuna:** Oh, Yui, could you display  
today's MMO Tomorrow  
for me?

<!-- uid=410400101:4:1 -->

**Yui:** Sure!

<!-- uid=410400101:5:1 -->

**Yui:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:6:1 -->

**Yui:** Download complete.  
Here you go, Daddy.

<!-- uid=410400101:7:1 -->

**Kirito:** Thanks, Yui.

<!-- uid=410400101:8:1 -->

**Kirito:** Now then, what's today's  
front-page story...?

<!-- uid=410400101:9:1 -->

**Asuna:** Come on, you two!  
Breakfast is ready.

<!-- uid=410400101:10:1 -->

**Kirito:** Uh-huh, cool, cool...  
Thanks, Asuna.

<!-- uid=410400101:11:1 -->

**Asuna:** Kirito, manners! Put the newspaper  
away at the breakfast table.  
It's rude!

<!-- uid=410400101:12:1 -->

**Kirito:** S-Sorry...  
Wait... What?!

<!-- uid=410400101:13:1 -->

**Asuna:** Kirito? What is it?

<!-- uid=410400101:14:1 -->

**Kirito:** It's an article about Jotunheimr,  
but...this looks like some  
seriously big news!

<!-- uid=410400101:15:1 -->

**Asuna:** Big news?

<!-- uid=410400101:16:1 -->

**Kirito:** Remember how the enemies there  
are so much stronger than the  
ones here on the surface?

<!-- uid=410400101:17:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yeah. It's so intense that you're  
sure to be wiped out soon unless  
you go with an advanced party.

<!-- uid=410400101:18:1 -->

**Kirito:** There's even a new crazy  
hack-and-slash quest  
there for masochists.

<!-- uid=410400101:19:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hack-and-slash for masochists?

<!-- uid=410400101:20:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah, since it's mainly  
the players getting  
hacked and slashed...

<!-- uid=410400101:21:1 -->

**Asuna:** Ha ha! Now I see  
what you mean.

<!-- uid=410400101:22:1 -->

**Yui:** Yeah! Daddy tried that quest  
on his own and nearly died!

<!-- uid=410400101:23:1 -->

**Asuna:** Y-You did what?!

<!-- uid=410400101:24:1 -->

**Kirito:** H-Hey, Yui! Didn't we agree not  
to tell Mommy about that...?

<!-- uid=410400101:25:1 -->

**Yui:** Oh, yeah...  
I'm sorry, Daddy.

<!-- uid=410400101:26:1 -->

**Asuna:** Agh, Kirito! Why are you always  
putting yourself in danger  
like that?

<!-- uid=410400101:27:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ha ha! I was just interested in  
seeing how tough it really was.

<!-- uid=410400101:28:1 -->

**Kirito:** And I did make it out in one  
piece... But it says somebody  
beat the whole quest alone!

<!-- uid=410400101:29:1 -->

**Asuna:** Huh?

<!-- uid=410400101:30:1 -->

**Kirito:** Imagine taking on a fight  
like that all by yourself-

<!-- uid=410400101:31:1 -->

**Yui:** You're one to talk, Daddy...

<!-- uid=410400101:32:1 -->

**Kirito:** But someone actually made it all  
the way! Whoever they are, they've  
got to be pretty incredible.

<!-- uid=410400101:33:1 -->

**Asuna:** Does the article mention  
that player's avatar name?

<!-- uid=410400101:34:1 -->

**Kirito:** No... It looks like they don't  
know it yet. All they have to go  
on so far are a few pictures.

<!-- uid=410400101:35:1 -->

**Asuna:** I see...

<!-- uid=410400101:36:1 -->

**Kirito:** I've got to figure out who it is!  
I'm going to look into this...

<!-- uid=410400101:37:1 -->

**Leafa:** And here we are!

<!-- uid=410400101:38:1 -->

**Sinon:** It's been ages since I was  
last in Jotunheimr.

<!-- uid=410400101:39:1 -->

**Kirito:** You two will be a real  
big help today!

<!-- uid=410400101:40:1 -->

**Asuna:** It's good to see you, Leafa!  
You too, Sinonon!

<!-- uid=410400101:41:1 -->

**Leafa:** So you're trying to find the  
mad genius who beat that  
nightmare-quest?

<!-- uid=410400101:42:1 -->

**Asuna:** That's the plan.

<!-- uid=410400101:43:1 -->

**Sinon:** It sure is odd. Think of the  
players who usually clear  
death-quests like this.

<!-- uid=410400101:44:1 -->

**Sinon:** You'd think they wouldn't stop  
bragging about it. Soon enough,  
everyone would know.

<!-- uid=410400101:45:1 -->

**Sinon:** But so far, nobody's taken credit.

<!-- uid=410400101:46:1 -->

**Kirito:** That's what you'd expect from  
most players. But maybe...

<!-- uid=410400101:47:1 -->

**Kirito:** Maybe it's not a player  
we're dealing with.

<!-- uid=410400101:48:1 -->

**Asuna:** Huh? You mean an NPC?

<!-- uid=410400101:49:1 -->

**Leafa:** So the NPC beating the quest was  
part of the storyline? Well,  
I guess that's possible...

<!-- uid=410400101:50:1 -->

**Sinon:** But that means there's a connection  
between players who've taken on the  
quest and that NPC, right?

<!-- uid=410400101:51:1 -->

**Asuna:** That's true. There have got to  
be players who have beaten the  
event.

<!-- uid=410400101:52:1 -->

**Sinon:** But we don't even know who  
those players could be...

<!-- uid=410400101:53:1 -->

**Sinon:** Is that even possible? Either way,  
I have a feeling they're trying to  
let people know how to beat it.

<!-- uid=410400101:54:1 -->

**Kirito:** I thought the same thing. That's  
one of the things I want to get  
to the bottom of.

<!-- uid=410400101:55:1 -->

**Asuna:** You know where the  
quest area is, right?

<!-- uid=410400101:56:1 -->

**Yui:** Yes. It's just beyond this wasteland.  
But you need to take out a monster  
vanguard to get there.

<!-- uid=410400101:57:1 -->

**Yui:** I think the person we're  
looking for could still  
be around here.

<!-- uid=410400101:58:1 -->

**Leafa:** Thanks, Yui.

<!-- uid=410400101:59:1 -->

**Kirito:** Alright, guys!  
Let's do this!

<!-- uid=410400101:60:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yeah!

<!-- uid=410400101:61:1 -->

**Leafa:** Ugh...  
I'm so exhausted...

<!-- uid=410400101:62:1 -->

**Sinon:** Leafa... This is a VRMMO.  
You can't get exhausted...

<!-- uid=410400101:63:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hahaha! You say that, Sinonon,  
but you're looking pretty worn  
out yourself...

<!-- uid=410400101:64:1 -->

**Sinon:** I'm just psychologically run down  
from that battle...

<!-- uid=410400101:65:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ha ha! Well, if you  
put it that way...

<!-- uid=410400101:66:1 -->

**Yui:** I hate to bring bad news, but  
that was only a tiny fraction  
of the enemy army...

<!-- uid=410400101:67:1 -->

**Leafa:** Huh?!  
No way!!

<!-- uid=410400101:68:2 -->

**Asuna:** Yes way... It's a good thing we  
were able to get out of that  
wasteland, though.

<!-- uid=410400101:69:1 -->

**Sinon:** Yeah. It's not so much that we  
defeated the enemy but more that we  
saw an opening and bravely ran away.

<!-- uid=410400101:70:1 -->

**Asuna:** Whoever was strong enough to  
take them all down must be  
incredible.

<!-- uid=410400101:71:1 -->

**Kirito:** I'll say. And they must be  
somewhere in these woods...

<!-- uid=410400101:72:1 -->

**Sinon:** Hey, look! There's smoke rising  
from that direction. There's got  
to be someone there, right?

<!-- uid=410400101:73:1 -->

**Kirito:** You're right!  
Let's check it out.

<!-- uid=410400101:74:1 -->

**???:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:75:1 -->

**???:** Huh...?!  
Who goes there?!

<!-- uid=410400101:76:1 -->

**Kirito:** Aha! There they are! So this is  
that legendary player...  
Or, um, NPC?

<!-- uid=410400101:77:1 -->

**Asuna:** It's a girl in armor...

<!-- uid=410400101:78:1 -->

**Leafa:** Wow...  
What a beautiful avatar.

<!-- uid=410400101:79:1 -->

**???:** Who goes there? You don't look like  
you're of the Forces of Darkness,  
but... State your business!

<!-- uid=410400101:80:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ah, we're sorry! My name is  
Kirito. I'm a Spriggan.

<!-- uid=410400101:81:1 -->

**Kirito:** And these are my friends  
Asuna, Leafa, and Sinon.

<!-- uid=410400101:82:1 -->

**???:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:83:1 -->

**Kirito:** Umm... Would it be all right to  
ask your name as well?

<!-- uid=410400101:84:1 -->

**???:** My name...?

<!-- uid=410400101:85:1 -->

**???:** My name is Alice...

<!-- uid=410400101:86:1 -->

**Alice:** I'm an Integrity Knight.  
Alice Synthesis Thirty.

<!-- uid=410400101:87:1 -->

**Kirito:** Alice... Synthesis?

<!-- uid=410400101:88:1 -->

**Leafa:** Wow, cool! I've never seen an  
avatar with a middle name in  
ALO before.

<!-- uid=410400101:89:1 -->

**Asuna:** She... She's a player...right?

<!-- uid=410400101:90:1 -->

**Sinon:** Well, we won't know unless we ask.

<!-- uid=410400101:91:1 -->

**Sinon:** Hey, Alice. How long have  
you been diving?

<!-- uid=410400101:92:1 -->

**Alice:** Diving...?

<!-- uid=410400101:93:1 -->

**Alice:** What in the world are  
you talking about?

<!-- uid=410400101:94:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...!

<!-- uid=410400101:95:1 -->

**Sinon:** Ah, so she's an NPC.

<!-- uid=410400101:96:1 -->

**Leafa:** Yeah. She doesn't have wings,  
and her design doesn't look  
quite like an ALO avatar.

<!-- uid=410400101:97:1 -->

**Asuna:** Seems that way...  
By the way, Alice,  
where are you from?

<!-- uid=410400101:98:1 -->

**Alice:** You've all been talking in some  
strange code since I  
first saw you... It's suspicious.

<!-- uid=410400101:99:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...!

<!-- uid=410400101:100:1 -->

**Asuna:** Oh! Um, we're sorry! We're, uh...  
We're from the surface.

<!-- uid=410400101:101:1 -->

**Asuna:** We heard that someone finally  
defeated the monsters here, so we  
came down to check it out.

<!-- uid=410400101:102:1 -->

**Alice:** The surface...?  
What are you talking about?

<!-- uid=410400101:103:1 -->

**Alice:** The light of the Sun God Solus  
blesses this land. Surely this  
is the surface.

<!-- uid=410400101:104:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:105:1 -->

**Sinon:** Hey, Kirito...

<!-- uid=410400101:106:1 -->

**Kirito:** R-Right... For an NPC, this  
girl's reactions are extremely  
complex...

<!-- uid=410400101:107:1 -->

**Kirito:** Then she must be a player  
after all... Or maybe...

<!-- uid=410400101:108:1 -->

**Alice:** It's true that Amayori and I were  
able to fend off the forces of  
the Dark Territory.

<!-- uid=410400101:109:1 -->

**Alice:** But as time went by, it seems they  
simply surged forth again from  
some other place.

<!-- uid=410400101:110:1 -->

**Kirito:** Dark Territory...?  
What's that?

<!-- uid=410400101:111:1 -->

**Alice:** ...We're not getting anywhere  
with this conversation, are we?

<!-- uid=410400101:112:1 -->

**Kirito:** Y-You can understand that too?

<!-- uid=410400101:113:1 -->

**Alice:** I hoped it wouldn't come to  
this, but... So be it. We'll  
have a trial by duel.

<!-- uid=410400101:114:1 -->

**Kirito:** What?!

<!-- uid=410400101:115:1 -->

**Alice:** After a single battle, I'll be  
able to tell whether you're  
minions of the Dark Territory.

<!-- uid=410400101:116:1 -->

**Kirito:** H-Hey, wait!  
We're not your enemies...  
I think!

<!-- uid=410400101:117:1 -->

**Alice:** But those pointed ears of yours...  
You certainly look like you're  
from the Dark Territory.

<!-- uid=410400101:118:1 -->

**Alice:** And you still haven't given me  
any reason to trust you.

<!-- uid=410400101:119:1 -->

**Kirito:** Dammit... Nothing you're  
saying makes sense!

<!-- uid=410400101:120:1 -->

**Alice:** Very well... I admit defeat.

<!-- uid=410400101:121:1 -->

**Alice:** You're not from the Dark Territory.  
A gentle spar can tell me that much  
from your sword style, at least.

<!-- uid=410400101:122:1 -->

**Kirito:** That was a gentle spar to you?!  
Wow, no wonder you managed to  
beat that quest by yourself...

<!-- uid=410400101:123:1 -->

**Asuna:** S-Stop!  
Alice, stop!

<!-- uid=410400101:124:1 -->

**Asuna:** We don't want to fight you!

<!-- uid=410400101:125:1 -->

**Alice:** I'm still not sure I trust you...  
But let's put away our weapons for  
now. I don't sense malice in you.

<!-- uid=410400101:126:1 -->

**Asuna:** Th-Thanks...

<!-- uid=410400101:127:1 -->

**Leafa:** We were thinking of taking on the  
same mon-Err, I mean, the "Forces  
of Darkness" that you defeated.

<!-- uid=410400101:128:1 -->

**Leafa:** So... I guess that  
makes us allies!

<!-- uid=410400101:129:1 -->

**Alice:** That may be true, but...you  
said you do not understand  
the words I use.

<!-- uid=410400101:130:1 -->

**Alice:** And you still look as though  
you don't trust me.

<!-- uid=410400101:131:1 -->

**Yui:** ...Daddy, if she's an NPC,  
then she's running  
on non-standard AI.

<!-- uid=410400101:132:1 -->

**Yui:** To be honest...  
She's even more advanced than me.

<!-- uid=410400101:133:1 -->

**Asuna:** M-More advanced than Yui...?  
Is that even possible in ALO?

<!-- uid=410400101:134:1 -->

**Yui:** That's all I can determine from  
our interactions so far...

<!-- uid=410400101:135:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:136:1 -->

**Kirito:** Hey, Alice. It's true that we  
can't understand everything  
you're saying...

<!-- uid=410400101:137:1 -->

**Kirito:** So how about we start over from  
the beginning? Can you tell us  
how you came here?

<!-- uid=410400101:138:1 -->

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:139:1 -->

**Alice:** Very well.

<!-- uid=410400101:140:1 -->

**Alice:** I am an Integrity Knight of the  
Axiom Church in Centoria.  
Sworn to protect the

<!-- uid=410400101:141:1 -->

**Alice:** Human Empire from the Dark  
Territory, I serve under the  
High Priest, the Administrator.

<!-- uid=410400101:142:1 -->

**Leafa:** You do what for the who-now?

<!-- uid=410400101:143:1 -->

**Kirito:** Wait, Sugu. Don't interrupt.  
Let's hear her out.

<!-- uid=410400101:144:1 -->

**Leafa:** S-Sure...

<!-- uid=410400101:145:1 -->

**Alice:** It began like any other  
night in Centoria.

<!-- uid=410400101:146:1 -->

**Alice:** I was riding my dragon Amayori,  
patrolling the skies above the  
Central Cathedral.

<!-- uid=410400101:147:1 -->

**Alice:** Then I saw an unmistakable  
silhouette against the moonlight:  
a Dark Knight riding dragon-back.

<!-- uid=410400101:148:1 -->

**Alice:** No intruder had ever made it  
so far into the capital. Sensing  
danger, I went to approach him.

<!-- uid=410400101:149:1 -->

**Alice:** But as soon as he saw me, he fled  
well beyond the boundaries of the  
Central Capital of Centoria.

<!-- uid=410400101:150:1 -->

**Alice:** Release Recollection!

<!-- uid=410400101:151:1 -->

**Dark Knight:** Gwaaargh....!!

<!-- uid=410400101:152:1 -->

**Alice:** And with that, the enemy  
was no more.

<!-- uid=410400101:153:1 -->

**Alice:** Come on, Amayori.  
We'd best get going.

<!-- uid=410400101:154:1 -->

**Amayori:** Grrroouh...

<!-- uid=410400101:155:1 -->

**Alice:** What's wrong, girl?  
Aww... You must be tired  
from all that flying.

<!-- uid=410400101:156:1 -->

**Alice:** Let's take a little rest  
on the ground.

<!-- uid=410400101:157:1 -->

**Amayori:** Grroou!

<!-- uid=410400101:158:1 -->

**Alice:** What...?  
What in the world is this?

<!-- uid=410400101:159:1 -->

**Alice:** All of a sudden, the air grew hazy.

<!-- uid=410400101:160:1 -->

**Alice:** Amayori! Do you know  
which way is home?!

<!-- uid=410400101:161:1 -->

**Amayori:** Ggruu.. Ggrouuuh!

<!-- uid=410400101:162:1 -->

**Alice:** You don't know either, huh...

<!-- uid=410400101:163:1 -->

**Alice:** Then we have no choice.  
We must land.

<!-- uid=410400101:164:1 -->

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:165:1 -->

**Amayori:** Ggrauuu...

<!-- uid=410400101:166:1 -->

**Alice:** Wh-What could this be?  
This strange sensation of  
being bathed in light...

<!-- uid=410400101:167:1 -->

**Alice:** What is this...?

<!-- uid=410400101:168:1 -->

**Alice:** Where are we?!

<!-- uid=410400101:169:1 -->

**Kirito:** So when you followed that light,  
you ended up in our world?

<!-- uid=410400101:170:1 -->

**Alice:** Right. So we kept walking, taking  
down any beings that seemed like  
agents of the Dark Territory...

<!-- uid=410400101:171:1 -->

**Alice:** After a few days, I happened  
upon a god of this world.

<!-- uid=410400101:172:1 -->

**Alice:** Let's rest at this spring for  
a while, Amayori...

<!-- uid=410400101:173:1 -->

**Amayori:** Grauu...!

<!-- uid=410400101:174:1 -->

**Urdr:** Who might you be?

<!-- uid=410400101:175:1 -->

**Alice:** Y-You're...!!

<!-- uid=410400101:176:1 -->

**Alice:** A giant woman...!  
Just like the ones I've read  
about in books of legends...!

<!-- uid=410400101:177:1 -->

**Urdr:** I am Urdr, Goddess of the Spring  
and Guardian of Jotunheimr.

<!-- uid=410400101:178:1 -->

**Alice:** Jotunheimr...?  
Goddess of the Spring?

<!-- uid=410400101:179:1 -->

**Urdr:** And what is your nature, my child?

<!-- uid=410400101:180:1 -->

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400101:181:1 -->

**Urdr:** Allow me to divine it, then.

<!-- uid=410400101:182:1 -->

**Urdr:** I sense in your being  
a powerful will to fight...

<!-- uid=410400101:183:1 -->

**Urdr:** I see... There's no doubt  
about it. Surely you must  
be a knight.

<!-- uid=410400101:184:1 -->

**Urdr:** As it so happens, I presently  
have need of a knight. Might  
you listen to my plea?

<!-- uid=410400101:185:1 -->

**Alice:** I... I'm sorry. I have something  
else that I must do. I don't  
have time for this.

<!-- uid=410400101:186:1 -->

**Urdr:** I see... What a pity. I thought  
I had finally found someone to  
fell those wicked brutes...

<!-- uid=410400101:187:1 -->

**Alice:** Brutes?  
You... You must mean the  
Dark Territory, right?!

<!-- uid=410400101:188:1 -->

**Urdr:** Whatever do you mean? They are the  
creatures from the world below  
this one, a world called Nibelheim.

<!-- uid=410400101:189:1 -->

**Alice:** Nibelheim? But a moment ago  
you said this place was called  
Jotunheimr...

<!-- uid=410400101:190:1 -->

**Alice:** Gaahh! I don't understand any  
of this! Are you trying to tell  
me this is another world?

<!-- uid=410400101:191:1 -->

**Alice:** Enough! How dare you try to  
deceive an Integrity Knight!

<!-- uid=410400101:192:1 -->

**Urdr:** You are a clearly a powerful knight  
with a noble heart and a piercing  
intellect...

<!-- uid=410400101:193:1 -->

**Urdr:** Surely, you must be able to  
appreciate the truth of what  
I say to you.

<!-- uid=410400101:194:1 -->

**Alice:** ...!

<!-- uid=410400101:195:1 -->

**Amayori:** Ggrauuu...

<!-- uid=410400101:196:1 -->

**Alice:** Amayori...  
You're right.

<!-- uid=410400101:197:1 -->

**Alice:** The form of this "Urdr" woman...  
She really does look like a  
goddess.

<!-- uid=410400101:198:1 -->

**Alice:** Perhaps she's a being on the  
same level as the Goddess Stacia.

<!-- uid=410400101:199:1 -->

**Alice:** Very well...  
Goddess of this realm.  
I will hear your request.

<!-- uid=410400101:200:1 -->

**Urdr:** Then you will listen?  
You have my thanks.

<!-- uid=410400101:201:1 -->

**Urdr:** I wish for you to gather  
a band of warriors as  
strong as yourself.

<!-- uid=410400101:202:1 -->

**Urdr:** Please work with them  
to save this realm.

<!-- uid=410400101:203:1 -->


## Story 410400201 · The Another World : Part 2

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

**Kirito:** What...?

<!-- uid=410400201:1:1 -->

**Leafa:** So Urdr asked you to come here  
and wipe out the Forces of  
Darkness... Is that right?

<!-- uid=410400201:2:1 -->

**Alice:** ...So I take it you know this  
goddess I speak of.

<!-- uid=410400201:3:1 -->

**Kirito:** Uh, yeah, we do.

<!-- uid=410400201:4:1 -->

**Alice:** The Goddess of the Spring asked me  
to gather the seven Divine Objects  
spread throughout this world...

<!-- uid=410400201:5:1 -->

**Alice:** And to find warriors strong  
enough to destroy the brutes  
disturbing her realm.

<!-- uid=410400201:6:1 -->

**Asuna:** K-Kirito... What's going on?

<!-- uid=410400201:7:1 -->

**Kirito:** If Alice really is an NPC, she  
must be describing the scenario  
for an event quest...

<!-- uid=410400201:8:1 -->

**Kirito:** But coming up with this level of  
detail for a minor character's  
backstory is just insane.

<!-- uid=410400201:9:1 -->

**Sinon:** No kidding. You'd never mistake  
her for a regular NPC...

<!-- uid=410400201:10:1 -->

**Kirito:** Maybe...  
Alice is...

<!-- uid=410400201:11:1 -->

**Alice:** So do you know anything about  
these Divine Objects or not?

<!-- uid=410400201:12:1 -->

**Leafa:** Divine Objects...?  
I haven't even heard of  
that item class before.

<!-- uid=410400201:13:1 -->

**Asuna:** Me neither... I know about the  
"Legendary" and "Ancient" item  
classes, but this is a new one...

<!-- uid=410400201:14:1 -->

**Alice:** In my world, Divine Objects are a  
set of extremely precious weapons  
and armor.

<!-- uid=410400201:15:1 -->

**Alice:** With them missing, I didn't really  
have much of a choice but to agree  
to help the goddess.

<!-- uid=410400201:16:1 -->

**Kirito:** Sorry we can't help that much.  
We don't know anything  
about them either...

<!-- uid=410400201:17:1 -->

**Alice:** What a shame...  
I heard a rumor that I might  
find one in this area.

<!-- uid=410400201:18:1 -->

**Alice:** But unless we find them and get  
back to our world soon...

<!-- uid=410400201:19:1 -->

from another world? Okay...

<!-- uid=410400201:159:1 -->

**Kirito:** Because no matter how you look at

and right now they need our help.

<!-- uid=410400201:160:1 -->

**Asuna:** Right!

<!-- uid=410400201:161:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400201:164:1 -->

**Kirito:** Fighting alongside these berserk  
warriors from another world...

<!-- uid=410400201:165:1 -->

**Kirito:** This is the kind of experience you  
could only access through a  
virtual world!

<!-- uid=410400201:166:1 -->

**Asuna:** Ha ha! I know what you mean...

<!-- uid=410400201:167:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah. And if there's just one

about those two...

<!-- uid=410400201:168:1 -->

**Kirito:** It's that they're good people!

somehow.

<!-- uid=410400201:169:1 -->

**Kirito:** I say we should team up with

<!-- uid=410400201:170:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yeah, I think so too!

let's do whatever we can to  
help them out.

<!-- uid=410400201:172:1 -->

**Kirito:** You bet!

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

<!-- uid=410400301:86:1 -->

**Asuna:** Alice, wait, let me cast my spell  
first so we can breathe...!

<!-- uid=410400301:87:1 -->

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400301:88:1 -->

**Asuna:** ...

<!-- uid=410400301:89:1 -->

**Yuuki:** We lost it... Guess there really  
wasn't any way we could keep  
up with its speed...

<!-- uid=410400301:90:1 -->

**Asuna:** And to make matters worse, we  
lost sight of the Object... Maybe it  
was eaten too...

<!-- uid=410400301:91:1 -->

**Alice:** I am to blame...

<!-- uid=410400301:92:1 -->

**Alice:** I am a poor swimmer to begin with,  
and my armor is only dragging me  
down...

<!-- uid=410400301:93:1 -->

**Alice:** It is obvious that I am the one  
who caused us to fail...

<!-- uid=410400301:94:1 -->

**Asuna:** It's not your fault, Alice! No one

attacked by a giant whale.

<!-- uid=410400301:95:1 -->

**Alice:** How can you be so calm, Asuna?

<!-- uid=410400301:96:1 -->

**Yuuki:** Don't worry, those two are  
totally still alive!

<!-- uid=410400301:97:1 -->

**Alice:** What...?

<!-- uid=410400301:98:1 -->

**Asuna:** Ummm, well, we're friends, so we  
can see their status-I mean, we  
can use a Stacia Window to check.

<!-- uid=410400301:99:1 -->

**Asuna:** See, if I do this, then we can see  
how Kirito and Eugeo are doing...

<!-- uid=410400301:100:1 -->

**Alice:** Ahh, indeed... Then those two are  
somehow still alive in the belly  
of that "whale" creature.

<!-- uid=410400301:101:1 -->

**Yuuki:** Exactly! So we can't abandon hope  
just yet. We've got work to do!

<!-- uid=410400301:102:1 -->

**Alice:** Understood. But before that,

<!-- uid=410400301:103:1 -->

**Asuna:** What is it, Alice?

<!-- uid=410400301:104:1 -->

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400301:105:1 -->

**Asuna:** How does it feel, Alice?

<!-- uid=410400301:106:1 -->

**Alice:** It is quite cool and comfortable.  
I should be able to swim more  
easily now, as well.

<!-- uid=410400301:107:1 -->

**Alice:** But there is such little fabric...

<!-- uid=410400301:108:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yeah, sorry... That's all I had

<!-- uid=410400301:109:1 -->

**Alice:** No need to apologize. This is what  
I must do to fulfill my duties, so  
I do not mind.

<!-- uid=410400301:110:1 -->

**Asuna:** Alright! Then let's go save the  
boys and find that Object!

<!-- uid=410400301:111:1 -->

**Yuuki:** Asuna! Alice!

<!-- uid=410400301:112:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hey, Yuuki's back!

<!-- uid=410400301:113:1 -->

**Yuuki:** I just spoke with some of those  
villagers.

<!-- uid=410400301:114:1 -->

**Yuuki:** They say that whale is called  
Leviathan, and it's been  
terrorizing these waters lately.

<!-- uid=410400301:115:1 -->

**Yuuki:** The poor fishermen don't know what  
to do.

<!-- uid=410400301:116:1 -->

**Asuna:** Leviathan is a monster from the  
Old Testament, isn't it?

<!-- uid=410400301:117:1 -->

**Alice:** Is that one of this world's myths?

<!-- uid=410400301:118:1 -->

**Asuna:** Um, yes... But people have  
speculated that it was inspired  
by real-life sperm whales.

<!-- uid=410400301:119:1 -->

**Alice:** A legendary monster is no foe to  
be taken lightly...

<!-- uid=410400301:120:1 -->

**Yuuki:** Absolutely! Luckily the villagers  
told me there's a way to take  
that whale down!

<!-- uid=410400301:121:1 -->

**Asuna:** That's great news! What do we  
have to do?

<!-- uid=410400301:122:1 -->

**Alice:** Tell us on the way. Time is of  
the essence.

<!-- uid=410400301:123:1 -->

**Yuuki:** You got it. Let's change into our  
swimsuits and go back to where  
we first saw that thing!

<!-- uid=410400301:124:1 -->

## Story 410400501 · The Star's Call : Part 1

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400501:1:1 -->

**Alice:** Is the climate in this region  
always so oppressive?

<!-- uid=410400501:2:1 -->

**Kirito:** I'm afraid so. Leprechauns built  
their territory in a swamp, after  
all.

<!-- uid=410400501:3:1 -->

**Kirito:** Bad weather, high humidity...  
It's not an easy place to live.

<!-- uid=410400501:4:1 -->

**Eugeo:** But we're closing in on a Divine  
Object, so it's not all bad.

<!-- uid=410400501:5:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah, that's true.

<!-- uid=410400501:6:1 -->

**Amayori:** Grrrr...

<!-- uid=410400501:7:1 -->

**Alice:** Seems we're not the only ones  
who find this place unbearable.

<!-- uid=410400501:8:1 -->

**Alice:** I shall permit Amayori to rest,  
seeing as we've arrived at our  
destination.

<!-- uid=410400501:9:1 -->

**Kirito:** Sounds good.

<!-- uid=410400501:10:1 -->

**Alice:** Let us find this Divine Object  
with all haste so we may leave  
this infernal place.

<!-- uid=410400501:11:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hehehe.

<!-- uid=410400501:12:1 -->

**Alice:** What has come over you, Asuna?

<!-- uid=410400501:13:1 -->

**Asuna:** I'm just glad to hear you say you  
want to go back to the house...

<!-- uid=410400501:14:1 -->

**Alice:** W-Well, I didn't quite mean it  
like that...

<!-- uid=410400501:15:1 -->

**Alice:** I was simply saying that I cannot  
abide this place.

<!-- uid=410400501:16:1 -->

**???:** Heeeey! Kiritoooo!

<!-- uid=410400501:17:1 -->

**Kirito:** That must be our welcome party!

<!-- uid=410400501:18:1 -->

**Liz:** Welcome to Leprechaun territory!

<!-- uid=410400501:19:1 -->

**Rain:** Thank you for making the journey  
in such foul weather. Oh, who am I  
kidding? It's always like this!

<!-- uid=410400501:20:1 -->

**Asuna:** Thank you for coming to greet us  
in this mess!

<!-- uid=410400501:21:1 -->

**Liz:** Oh, please! It's the least we  
could do after you came all  
this way.

<!-- uid=410400501:22:1 -->

**Liz:** Now, let's ditch the formalities  
so I can show you around our  
home turf as a friend!

<!-- uid=410400501:23:1 -->

**Kirito:** That'd be great.

<!-- uid=410400501:24:1 -->

**Alice:** Liz and Rain... So you're Kirito's  
Leprechaun compatriots...

<!-- uid=410400501:25:1 -->

**Eugeo:** They'll be assisting us in our  
search for the Divine Object  
in this region.

<!-- uid=410400501:26:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yep!

<!-- uid=410400501:27:1 -->

**Liz:** And guess what!  
We already know where it is.

<!-- uid=410400501:28:1 -->

**Alice:** You do?!

<!-- uid=410400501:29:1 -->

**Kirito:** Wow, really?

<!-- uid=410400501:30:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Incredible! We'll be finished here  
in no time flat!

<!-- uid=410400501:31:1 -->

**Kirito:** So where is it?

<!-- uid=410400501:32:1 -->

**Rain:** It's in a vault on the Leprechaun  
lord's premises.

<!-- uid=410400501:33:1 -->

**Kirito:** What!

<!-- uid=410400501:34:1 -->

**Asuna:** Oh no...

<!-- uid=410400501:35:1 -->

**Liz:** Oh no is right...

<!-- uid=410400501:36:1 -->

**Liz:** In a way, this might be your  
toughest challenge yet.

<!-- uid=410400501:37:1 -->

**Alice:** What does she mean by that?

<!-- uid=410400501:38:1 -->

**Kirito:** Basically that someone else beat  
us to the Divine Object in this  
territory.

<!-- uid=410400501:39:1 -->

**Kirito:** I'd ask how tightly it's being  
guarded, but I think I already  
know the answer...

<!-- uid=410400501:40:1 -->

**Rain:** Remember, we're widely known  
as a race of blacksmiths...

<!-- uid=410400501:41:1 -->

**Rain:** We know our stuff when it comes  
to items and materials.

<!-- uid=410400501:42:1 -->

**Liz:** Especially when they're all anyone  
can talk about, like these Divine  
Objects.

<!-- uid=410400501:43:1 -->

**Rain:** And all they ever say is how  
unique and incredible it must be.

<!-- uid=410400501:44:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ugh, this is bad... Very bad...

<!-- uid=410400501:45:3 -->

**Asuna:** I know...

<!-- uid=410400501:46:1 -->

**Alice:** What's so bad about safeguarding  
that which is yours? Surely we can  
negotiate.

<!-- uid=410400501:47:1 -->

**Liz:** Well, you see, Alice, we  
Leprechauns are really, really  
glad we have this thing.

<!-- uid=410400501:48:1 -->

**Liz:** So we won't just hand it over.

<!-- uid=410400501:49:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Not even if we give you a bunch of  
money? You call it Yuld, right?

<!-- uid=410400501:50:1 -->

**Rain:** Yeah, I don't think you have that  
kind of money.

<!-- uid=410400501:51:1 -->

**Alice:** Suppose we find an item equal in  
value and arrange a trade?

<!-- uid=410400501:52:1 -->

**Liz:** It's easy for you to say that,  
but I can't think of another  
item as rare as this one.

<!-- uid=410400501:53:1 -->

**Alice:** Well then. Should negotiations

<!-- uid=410400601:123:1 -->

**Leprechaun A:** I did.

<!-- uid=410400601:124:1 -->

**Leprechaun A:** She could have easily left me to  
die and made off with the Object,  
but she saved me instead.

<!-- uid=410400601:125:1 -->

**Leprechaun A:** It was then that I saw monsters  
crawling up from the swamp as  
light emanated from the Object.

<!-- uid=410400601:126:1 -->

**Leprechaun B:** That is rather incontrovertible  
proof.

<!-- uid=410400601:127:1 -->

**Leprechaun C:** Agreed. I believe we have no  
choice but to acknowledge  
they speak the truth.

<!-- uid=410400601:128:1 -->

**Kirito:** You did it, Alice!

<!-- uid=410400601:129:1 -->

**Asuna:** That was incredible!

<!-- uid=410400601:130:1 -->

**Alice:** I did not do it for your  
accolades.

<!-- uid=410400601:131:1 -->

**Alice:** I did it merely to protect my  
pride as an Integrity Knight.

<!-- uid=410400601:132:1 -->

**Kirito:** And we wouldn't have even made it  
this far without Eugeo's theory.

<!-- uid=410400601:133:1 -->

**Kirito:** You two were definitely our MVPs  
this time around.

<!-- uid=410400601:134:1 -->

**Eugeo:** I'm happy to take the compliment,  
but I contributed very little.

<!-- uid=410400601:135:1 -->

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400601:136:1 -->

**Rain:** Are you sure this is what  
you want? You know if you  
take the Object with you,

<!-- uid=410400601:137:1 -->

**Rain:** your region will be crawling with  
powerful monsters, right?

<!-- uid=410400601:138:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah, you're right. We'll have to  
come up with a strategy.

<!-- uid=410400601:139:1 -->

**Alice:** That won't be necessary.  
Crushing the Forces of  
Darkness shall suffice.

<!-- uid=410400601:140:1 -->

**Asuna:** Ha ha! Still, even so...

<!-- uid=410400601:141:1 -->

**Asuna:** Why do Urdr's Divine Objects  
attract these monsters?

<!-- uid=410400601:142:1 -->

**Eugeo:** I suspect it's due to the immense  
power they contain, but perhaps  
you have a different theory?

<!-- uid=410400601:143:1 -->

**Asuna:** I'd call it more of a hunch...

<!-- uid=410400601:144:1 -->

**Liz:** So, care to take this Divine  
Object off our hands?

<!-- uid=410400601:145:1 -->

**Rain:** Ha ha! I think there'll be some  
sad faces in Leprechaun territory  
tonight...

<!-- uid=410400601:146:1 -->

**Kirito:** Hah, sorry about that.  
We'll be on our way, then.

<!-- uid=410400601:147:1 -->

**Kirito:** Take care of yourselves, okay?

<!-- uid=410400601:148:1 -->

**Liz:** We will. See you later!

<!-- uid=410400601:149:1 -->


## Story 410400701 · The Malefic Star : Part 1

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

**Alice:** ...

<!-- uid=410400701:1:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:2:1 -->

**Alice:** No. Should I be?

<!-- uid=410400701:3:1 -->

time we've been trolled.

**Alice:** Trolled?

<!-- uid=410400701:5:1 -->

we got.

<!-- uid=410400701:6:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:7:1 -->

The whole story was a fake.

<!-- uid=410400701:8:1 -->

world?!

<!-- uid=410400701:9:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:10:1 -->

information brokers, and they buy  
and sell rumors for cash.

<!-- uid=410400701:11:1 -->

**Kirito:** Some of them are trustworthy,  
but some of them spread false info  
just for the laughs.

<!-- uid=410400701:12:1 -->

blackguards and strike them down.  
Evil will not be tolerated.

<!-- uid=410400701:13:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:14:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:15:1 -->

**Asuna:** Wait, what?!

<!-- uid=410400701:16:1 -->

**Kirito:** That's not Aincrad!

<!-- uid=410400701:17:1 -->

familiar with.

<!-- uid=410400701:18:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Agreed. It's still far away,  
and it's hard to make out  
in this light...

<!-- uid=410400701:19:1 -->

**Eugeo:** It's too small, and something

<!-- uid=410400701:20:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:22:1 -->

**Yui:** Aincrad was replaced with another

<!-- uid=410400701:23:1 -->

**Kirito:** Okay, sure. I'm definitely getting

this place.

<!-- uid=410400701:24:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:25:1 -->

their ilk?!

<!-- uid=410400701:26:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Did they use some form of foul  
sorcery to destroy Aincrad?!

<!-- uid=410400701:27:1 -->

**Alice:** Your home... It's gone...

**Kirito:** Yeah, it's gone. However, I bet if

will return safe and sound.

<!-- uid=410400701:29:1 -->

**Kirito:** Let's take a minute to get ready,  
then bust into that bastion!

<!-- uid=410400701:30:1 -->

the Divine Object though.

<!-- uid=410400701:31:1 -->

**Alice:** We have no choice if we

<!-- uid=410400701:32:1 -->

**Yui:** It appears to be another one of

<!-- uid=410400701:36:1 -->

**Kirito:** We can't really just say,

**Kirito:** Yeah!

<!-- uid=410400701:52:1 -->

**Demon:** Graaargh!

<!-- uid=410400701:53:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:60:1 -->

**Alice:** I...have no words.

the gift of speech.

**Alice:** Repulsive. I shall cleanse this

<!-- uid=410400701:66:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:70:1 -->

**Kirito:** But at this rate, I'll need to

<!-- uid=410400701:71:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:72:1 -->

**Asuna:** And we can't let that Divine

<!-- uid=410400701:73:1 -->

**Kirito:** No, we definitely can't!

<!-- uid=410400701:74:1 -->

**Werewolf:** Awoooooo!!

<!-- uid=410400701:75:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:77:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:78:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Ahahaha, this is great!

This is no time for laughter!

<!-- uid=410400701:80:1 -->

**Kirito:** R-Right, I'm coming!

<!-- uid=410400701:82:1 -->

**Ghost:** Booooo...

<!-- uid=410400701:83:1 -->

**Asuna:** Wah!

<!-- uid=410400701:84:1 -->

<!-- uid=410400701:86:1 -->

**Asuna:** I just freaked out for a second

<!-- uid=410400701:87:1 -->

**Asuna:** But I don't mind this ghost that

<!-- uid=410400701:88:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ha ha, yeah. It's good to know

trigger your phantom phobia!

<!-- uid=410400701:89:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hehe, yeah!

<!-- uid=410400701:90:1 -->

**Ghost:** RRRWAAAAAAGHHHHHH!!!

<!-- uid=410400701:91:1 -->

**Asuna:** Awaagh?!

<!-- uid=410400701:92:1 -->

**Kirito:** Whoa! It may look cute, but its

about...

<!-- uid=410400701:93:1 -->

**Asuna:** Y-Yeah... It's not scary, but  
that doesn't mean it's not  
a threat... I'll be more careful.

<!-- uid=410400701:94:1 -->

**Kirito:** Good idea. All the monsters here  
are actually pretty tough.

<!-- uid=410400701:95:1 -->

**Kirito:** Hmm... Hold up!

<!-- uid=410400701:96:1 -->

**Asuna:** What is it, Kirito?

<!-- uid=410400701:97:1 -->

**Kirito:** Guess what, Asuna?  
I think I've figured out this  
castle's dark secret...

<!-- uid=410400701:98:1 -->

**Alice:** Is the left corridor clear?

<!-- uid=410400701:99:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yup, Kirito and I handled it.  
Everything okay over here?

<!-- uid=410400701:100:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Yeah, somehow. But wow...  
There's a lot of strange and  
surprising monsters in here, huh?

<!-- uid=410400701:101:1 -->

**Eugeo:** This might sound odd, but I'm  
having fun! I feel like I see  
something new around every corner!

<!-- uid=410400701:102:1 -->

**Alice:** Please, can you talk some sense  
into him? He's been acting like  
this is for the past hour.

<!-- uid=410400701:103:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ha ha... Has he, now...?

<!-- uid=410400701:104:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Sorry, Alice. I'll be more serious  
from here on out. Especially after  
what just happened.

<!-- uid=410400701:105:1 -->

**Asuna:** After what? What happened?

<!-- uid=410400701:106:1 -->

**Alice:** We ran into another party.  
They were fairies, like you.

<!-- uid=410400701:107:1 -->

**Kirito:** Really?!

<!-- uid=410400701:108:1 -->

**Kirito:** I can't believe another group  
managed to make it this far  
already.

<!-- uid=410400701:109:1 -->

**Eugeo:** They were fierce warriors,  
and strong. We can't let ourselves  
grow complacent.

<!-- uid=410400701:110:1 -->

**Alice:** Do you think they're after the  
Divine Object too?

<!-- uid=410400701:111:1 -->

**Kirito:** Most likely. It looks like we'll  
have to pick up the pace.

<!-- uid=410400701:112:1 -->

**Kirito:** If someone beats us to the punch  
like back in Leprechaun territory,  
it'll be a big pain.

<!-- uid=410400701:113:1 -->

**Kirito:** Come on! Let's get a move on!

<!-- uid=410400701:114:1 -->

**Alice:** Hyaaah!

<!-- uid=410400701:115:1 -->

**Demon:** Grrack!

<!-- uid=410400701:116:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Alice, you finished over there?

<!-- uid=410400701:117:1 -->

**Alice:** Of course. No evil can withstand  
my righteous wrath.

<!-- uid=410400701:118:1 -->

**Alice:** Now, let's regroup with Kirito and  
Asuna before we challenge the  
next set of enemies.

<!-- uid=410400701:119:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Yeah, that's a-huh?

<!-- uid=410400701:120:1 -->

**Player:** Hey, fancy seeing you two again.

<!-- uid=410400701:121:1 -->

**Alice:** I remember you from before...

<!-- uid=410400701:122:1 -->

**Player:** Looks like you guys are making  
pretty good progress yourselves.

<!-- uid=410400701:123:1 -->

**Player:** We'll have to hurry up before  
you beat us to the reward.

<!-- uid=410400701:124:1 -->

**Eugeo:** You plan on claiming the Divine  
Object as your own?

<!-- uid=410400701:125:1 -->

**Player:** Divine Object...? You mean the  
quest reward? Yeah, for sure.  
It looks super rare.

<!-- uid=410400701:126:1 -->

**Alice:** This tale of a great reward  
awaiting he who cleanses this  
castle... It IS true, right?

<!-- uid=410400701:127:1 -->

**Eugeo:** A-Alice? But-

<!-- uid=410400701:128:1 -->

**Alice:** I trust Kirito. Nevertheless, our  
enemies could be deceiving him  
too. It's worth confirming.

<!-- uid=410400701:129:1 -->

**Eugeo:** R-Right... Good thinking...

<!-- uid=410400701:130:1 -->

**Player:** The info on the reward?  
Well, yeah... I'm pretty sure  
it's legit.

<!-- uid=410400701:131:1 -->

**Player:** I mean, it was an official  
announcement. They don't  
fake that stuff.

<!-- uid=410400701:132:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Phew, that's a relief. It looks  
like this time our information  
is trustworthy.

<!-- uid=410400701:133:1 -->

**Alice:** Yes, that is a relief. However...  
I feel we should tell them what  
we know of the Divine Objects.

<!-- uid=410400701:134:1 -->

**Eugeo:** You mean-

<!-- uid=410400701:135:1 -->

**Alice:** If we do, then perhaps they'll  
give up trying to claim it.

<!-- uid=410400701:136:1 -->

**Eugeo:** ...Yes, you're right. But Kirito  
told us we should try to avoid  
talking to other fairies...

<!-- uid=410400701:137:1 -->

**Eugeo:** What he says makes sense.  
We're from another world, after  
all. It could cause problems.

<!-- uid=410400701:138:1 -->

**Alice:** True... I agree that we should  
avoid unnecessary contact  
wherever possible.

<!-- uid=410400701:139:1 -->

**Alice:** Perhaps we should confer with  
Kirito and Asuna before we take  
any further action.

<!-- uid=410400701:140:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Yeah, I think that's wise. I bet  
they would handle it better than  
us, anyway.

<!-- uid=410400701:141:1 -->

**Alice:** Understood.  
You there. We apologize for taking  
up your time. We will withdraw.

<!-- uid=410400701:142:1 -->

**Player:** Uh...sure?  
Good luck, and see you around.

<!-- uid=410400701:143:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...

<!-- uid=410400701:144:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hey Kirito... Now that Alice and  
Eugeo aren't here, there's  
something I want to talk about.

<!-- uid=410400701:145:1 -->

**Asuna:** The Divine Object's curse created  
this whole castle, didn't it?

<!-- uid=410400701:146:1 -->

**Kirito:** It's not one hundred percent  
certain, but it is very likely.

<!-- uid=410400701:147:1 -->

**Asuna:** B-But...the curse usually just  
summons hordes of powerful  
monsters, right?

<!-- uid=410400701:148:1 -->

**Asuna:** I find it hard to believe it's  
strong enough to summon a  
whole castle out of thin air...

<!-- uid=410400701:149:1 -->

**Yui:** I have a question. Are you using  
the amulet Liz gave you?

<!-- uid=410400701:150:1 -->

**Yui:** I believe it was supposed to  
weaken the curse's power.

<!-- uid=410400701:151:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah, that's what we thought,  
but I guess it's run out of juice.  
I don't think it's working.

<!-- uid=410400701:152:1 -->

**Kirito:** But there's something that bothers  
me even more about all of this.

<!-- uid=410400701:153:1 -->

**Kirito:** Why is a Divine Object the reward  
for the Halloween event in the  
first place?

<!-- uid=410400701:154:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hey, that's a good point!

<!-- uid=410400701:155:1 -->

**Kirito:** Our working theory right now is  
that people from Alice's world  
stressed the system when they

<!-- uid=410400701:156:1 -->

**Kirito:** came here, and Cardinal tried to  
cope by creating the Divine  
Objects.

<!-- uid=410400701:157:1 -->

**Kirito:** So isn't it strange that one is  
now the Halloween event's unique  
reward?

<!-- uid=410400701:158:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yeah. We assumed that only people  
partying with Alice and Eugeo-us-  
could do Divine Object quests.

<!-- uid=410400701:159:1 -->

**Asuna:** But if one of them is a public  
event's reward, that shoots a big  
fat hole in our theory, huh?

<!-- uid=410400701:160:1 -->

**Kirito:** Come to think of it, other players  
were able to mess with our quest  
in the Leprechaun territory too.

<!-- uid=410400701:161:1 -->

**Kirito:** Maybe the rules governing the  
Divine Objects have changed  
since Alice and Eugeo appeared?

<!-- uid=410400701:162:1 -->

**Asuna:** Changed? What do you mean?

<!-- uid=410400701:163:1 -->

**Kirito:** The longer Alice and Eugeo are  
here, the more entangled they  
become with our world.

<!-- uid=410400701:164:1 -->

**Kirito:** Perhaps the system is converting  
their data. Turning them native,  
so to speak.

<!-- uid=410400701:165:1 -->

**Asuna:** But... Isn't that dangerous for  
them?

<!-- uid=410400701:166:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah, I think so. If the process  
goes far enough, they might never  
be able to return home...

<!-- uid=410400701:167:1 -->

**Asuna:** We can't let that happen!

<!-- uid=410400701:168:1 -->

**Kirito:** Well, remember. This is just a  
wild guess. Let's not mention it  
to them until we have proof.

<!-- uid=410400701:169:1 -->

**Asuna:** Y-Yeah... Well, there are seven  
Divine Objects, and we have three  
now...

<!-- uid=410400701:170:1 -->

**Asuna:** Let's hurry up and find the other  
four.

<!-- uid=410400701:171:1 -->

**Kirito:** Hmmm... Yeah, this might end up  
being a big waste of time...

<!-- uid=410400701:172:1 -->

**Kirito:** But I think we should go check  
that area out, just in case.

<!-- uid=410400701:173:1 -->

**Asuna:** You think we'll find Urdr there?

<!-- uid=410400701:174:1 -->

**Kirito:** Maybe. I do know I've got a lot of  
questions for her. What these  
Divine Objects are, for starters.

<!-- uid=410400701:175:1 -->


## Story 410400801 · The Malefic Star : Part 2

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

**Alice:** Kirito! Asuna!  
I demand an explanation!

there.

hands on the Divine Object too,  
and they were pushing hard.

this, but I'd very much appreciate  
you keeping us in the loop.

<!-- uid=410400801:5:1 -->

**Kirito:** I'm sorry... It's not that I don't  
want to tell you.

the words...

<!-- uid=410400801:7:1 -->

**Kirito:** I know this isn't exactly fair to  
you, but I hope you forgive me.

<!-- uid=410400801:8:1 -->

**Asuna:** Kirito...

## Story 410401001 · The Castle Demon : Part 2

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

**Asuna:** Hnngh...

<!-- uid=410401001:1:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401001:3:1 -->

limited-time ailment only  
available for Halloween.

**Kirito:** Fitting.

<!-- uid=410401001:5:1 -->

**Yui:** Its effects are temporary, but it  
is impossible to use any existing  
items to cure it.

**Kirito:** then something had to have the  
power to alter their default  
behavior patterns.

<!-- uid=410401101:170:1 -->

**Seven:** I see what you're getting at.  
The monsters are following  
someONE's orders, right?

<!-- uid=410401101:171:1 -->

**Seven:** That's the only explanation for  
why the system would change  
their behavior to attack us.

<!-- uid=410401101:172:1 -->

**Kirito:** Exactly. And if we follow this  
thread to its logical conclusion,  
there's only one possibility.

<!-- uid=410401101:173:1 -->

**Asuna:** Alice and Eugeo's Cait Sith NPC?

<!-- uid=410401101:174:1 -->

**Kirito:** No, that's not entirely right.  
If there's a Cait Sith ordering  
these monsters to attack us...

<!-- uid=410401101:175:1 -->

**Kirito:** It's probably not an NPC...  
But a player!!

<!-- uid=410401101:176:1 -->

**Seven:** Ahhh, that makes sense! Even an

couldn't issue an order like that.

<!-- uid=410401101:177:1 -->

**Asuna:** But why would a player even do  
that?

<!-- uid=410401101:178:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:11:1 -->

**Alice:** He fled into that black void!  
Quickly now, after him!

<!-- uid=410401301:12:1 -->

**Alice:** What?!

<!-- uid=410401301:14:1 -->

**Kirito:** Alice!

<!-- uid=410401301:15:1 -->

**Asuna:** Alice!

<!-- uid=410401301:16:1 -->

**Alice:** I...can't enter this strange  
void?

<!-- uid=410401301:17:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...

<!-- uid=410401301:18:1 -->

**Kirito:** I don't think we can either.

<!-- uid=410401301:19:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Just what is this thing?

<!-- uid=410401301:20:1 -->

**Asuna:** It's a portal to  
another place, Eugeo, but one  
that only he can use.

<!-- uid=410401301:21:1 -->

**Kirito:** Apparently...

<!-- uid=410401301:22:1 -->

**Alice:** There's only two Divine Objects  
left, and we know now that this  
Cait Sith has one of them...

<!-- uid=410401301:23:1 -->

**Alice:** I can't believe we let him  
escape us like that...

<!-- uid=410401301:24:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Well, we tried... Huh?

<!-- uid=410401301:25:1 -->

**Kirito:** What is it, Eugeo?

<!-- uid=410401301:26:1 -->

**Eugeo:** I can see something on the other  
side of that black void!

<!-- uid=410401301:27:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hey, you're right!  
I think it's a town!

<!-- uid=410401301:28:1 -->

**Kirito:** It definitely is a town, but it

in Alfheim. It's very...Japanese.

<!-- uid=410401301:29:1 -->

**Alice:** Japanese...?

<!-- uid=410401301:30:1 -->

**Kirito:** Oh, uh...how do I explain this...?

from a different culture.

<!-- uid=410401301:31:1 -->

**Asuna:** Maybe it's a special event area,

<!-- uid=410401301:32:1 -->

**Kirito:** But I haven't heard of any

happening in ALO now...

<!-- uid=410401301:33:1 -->

**Kirito:** Hmm... No, it couldn't be...

<!-- uid=410401301:34:1 -->

**Asuna:** Couldn't be what, Kirito?

<!-- uid=410401301:35:1 -->

**Kazuto:** Looks like we're all ready to go.

<!-- uid=410401301:36:1 -->

**Asuna:** I'm just glad we were able to  
format Alice and Eugeo's data so  
they could be converted over.

<!-- uid=410401301:37:1 -->

**Kazuto:** They were pretty suspicious about

<!-- uid=410401301:38:1 -->

**Asuna:** And they were right to be. Not  
like they would've believed us if  
we said we're switching VRMMOs...

<!-- uid=410401301:39:1 -->

**Kazuto:** Yeah, that wouldn't have gone

<!-- uid=410401301:40:1 -->

**Asuna:** But...did that Cait Sith really

<!-- uid=410401301:41:1 -->

**Kazuto:** Well, you saw it, right? That town

was a Japanese-themed VRMMO.

<!-- uid=410401301:42:1 -->

**Kazuto:** And it's got to be Asuka Empire.

<!-- uid=410401301:43:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yuuki used to play Asuka Empire,

<!-- uid=410401301:44:1 -->

**Kazuto:** Yeah. It's connected to the Seed  
Nexus, just like ALO, so data can  
be transferred between the two.

<!-- uid=410401301:45:1 -->

**Kazuto:** Player character data or NPC.

<!-- uid=410401301:46:1 -->

**Asuna:** I suppose that makes sense...

<!-- uid=410401301:47:1 -->

**Asuna:** Still, I'm amazed he thought to  
jump VRMMOs. Cardinal really

<!-- uid=410401301:48:1 -->

**Kazuto:** But that's also his weakness.  
Cardinal based that Cait Sith off  
Alice and Eugeo's data, right?

<!-- uid=410401301:49:1 -->

**Kazuto:** Well, their minds work just like  
ours. In other words, AI or not,  
he's got human limitations.

<!-- uid=410401301:50:1 -->

**Kazuto:** We just need to predict his next  
move, set a trap, and corner him.  
Easy as that.

<!-- uid=410401301:51:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yeah, you're right.  
Well, let's get to it!  
To a new world!

<!-- uid=410401301:52:1 -->

**Asuna:** I bet Alice and Eugeo can't wait

<!-- uid=410401301:53:1 -->

**Kazuto:** Yeah!

<!-- uid=410401301:54:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...

<!-- uid=410401301:55:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:56:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:57:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:58:1 -->

**Kirito:** Let me just check the class

**Alice:** Where...are we?

<!-- uid=410401301:60:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Wow! This world is so...bizarre!

<!-- uid=410401301:61:1 -->

**Asuna:** Good morning, guys!

<!-- uid=410401301:62:1 -->

**Alice:** You are...Asuna? Your hair is a

<!-- uid=410401301:63:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:64:1 -->

**Alice:** Mix...what up?

<!-- uid=410401301:65:1 -->

**Asuna:** Well... I thought like changing my

<!-- uid=410401301:66:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Hmm, you both are wearing pretty

<!-- uid=410401301:67:1 -->

**Kirito:** Hehe. I look pretty cool, right?

<!-- uid=410401301:68:1 -->

**Alice:** Kirito, the change in your

<!-- uid=410401301:69:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ouch...

<!-- uid=410401301:70:1 -->

void? Are we no longer in Alfheim?

<!-- uid=410401301:71:2 -->

**Kirito:** Y-Yeah, that's right. This world  
is completely separate from  
Alfheim.

<!-- uid=410401301:72:1 -->

**Kirito:** It has its own history and

<!-- uid=410401301:73:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Well, I like it here! It seems

<!-- uid=410401301:74:1 -->

**Alice:** Eugeo, this is not a leisurely

<!-- uid=410401301:75:1 -->

**Eugeo:** S-Sorry, Alice. I know...  
We're here to find that  
cloaked Cait Sith...

<!-- uid=410401301:76:1 -->

**Asuna:** About that...  
When he converted...

<!-- uid=410401301:77:1 -->

**Asuna:** I mean, when he came to this  
world, he may have changed  
his appearance.

<!-- uid=410401301:78:1 -->

**Eugeo:** He can do that?

<!-- uid=410401301:79:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah, and it's likely he assumed

to blend in.

<!-- uid=410401301:80:1 -->

**Kirito:** Hmm... Maybe disguising himself

<!-- uid=410401301:81:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:82:1 -->

**Kirito:** Exactly. Hey, I've got an idea.

let's give him a new name.

<!-- uid=410401301:83:1 -->

**Kirito:** Highly Intelligent Mob,

<!-- uid=410401301:84:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Hi-Mob? What's that supposed

<!-- uid=410401301:85:1 -->

**Asuna:** It means "a very smart enemy."  
Since we don't know what he looks  
like anymore,

<!-- uid=410401301:86:1 -->

**Asuna:** I guess that's a good enough way

<!-- uid=410401301:87:1 -->

**Alice:** Understood. From here on, we shall

<!-- uid=410401301:88:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:93:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:94:1 -->

**Eugeo:** It's like a festival!

**Kirito:** Well, I don't think it's "like"

<!-- uid=410401301:96:1 -->

**Kirito:** Asuka Empire's right in the middle

<!-- uid=410401301:97:1 -->

**Eugeo:** It's New Year's here?

<!-- uid=410401301:98:1 -->

**Asuna:** Yup! And they apparently celebrate

<!-- uid=410401301:99:1 -->

**Alice:** I see. That explains why they are

<!-- uid=410401301:100:1 -->

**Kirito:** About what?

**Kirito:** Good point...

<!-- uid=410401301:104:1 -->

**Asuna:** Do you think that's why he came

<!-- uid=410401301:105:1 -->

**Kirito:** I wouldn't be surprised. He really

<!-- uid=410401301:106:1 -->

**Kirito:** Converting let him change his

holiday crowd.

<!-- uid=410401301:107:1 -->

**Kirito:** He's way smarter than we

<!-- uid=410401301:108:1 -->

**Asuna:** Well, I think we won't have to

or tricks.

<!-- uid=410401301:109:1 -->

**Kirito:** Why do you say that, Asuna?

**Asuna:** He came here so that he could hide

<!-- uid=410401301:111:1 -->

**Asuna:** But I highly doubt he had the time  
to come up with an even sneakier  
or more ingenious plan.

<!-- uid=410401301:112:1 -->

**Asuna:** My guess is, he found the most  
crowded place in town and is  
hiding out there.

<!-- uid=410401301:113:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ahh, interesting theory!

<!-- uid=410401301:114:1 -->

**Alice:** That detective seemed extremely  
unreliable. Do you really think we

<!-- uid=410401301:115:1 -->

**Asuna:** The Mitsuba Detective Agency has  
a good reputation, so I think so.

<!-- uid=410401301:116:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Well, we know that there's a big,  
traditional game tournament going  
on today in Ayakashi Alley.

<!-- uid=410401301:117:1 -->

**Kirito:** Supposedly, it's karuta. That game  
is the most popular one here  
during the New Year's season.

<!-- uid=410401301:118:1 -->

**Alice:** What is this "karuta" you speak of?

<!-- uid=410401301:119:1 -->

**Asuna:** It's a game you play with picture  
cards. It's sort of a tradition in  
Japan... I mean, the Asuka Empire.

<!-- uid=410401301:120:1 -->

**Asuna:** The rules are pretty simple, so  
it's quite easy to pick up.

<!-- uid=410401301:121:1 -->

**Alice:** I have no desire to waste my time  
playing this game.

<!-- uid=410401301:122:1 -->

**Kirito:** Well, hold on a sec. It might be  
a good idea to join the karuta  
tournament.

<!-- uid=410401301:123:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Why?

<!-- uid=410401301:124:1 -->

**Kirito:** I bet you our elusive enemy is  
in Ayakashi Alley. Either watching  
the tournament,

<!-- uid=410401301:125:1 -->

**Kirito:** or playing himself. What better  
place for him to hide in plain  
sight than on stage?

<!-- uid=410401301:126:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hmm, that kinda makes sense. We

in the spotlight.

<!-- uid=410401301:127:1 -->

**Kirito:** Exactly. So we join the tournament  
and give each and every player a  
close look over. Sound good?

<!-- uid=410401301:128:1 -->

**Alice:** Kirito... You have purposefully

<!-- uid=410401301:129:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:130:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Alice has a very good point.  
Every time you go somewhere new,

<!-- uid=410401301:131:1 -->

**Eugeo:** you just HAVE to try anything

<!-- uid=410401301:132:1 -->

**Kirito:** H-Hey, hold it! Priorities! I have

Object comes first!

<!-- uid=410401301:133:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hehe... You two have learned how  
to read Kirito pretty well. I'd say  
you're on to something.

<!-- uid=410401301:134:1 -->

**Kirito:** Asuna, you too? Give me a break!

<!-- uid=410401301:135:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Ahaha!

<!-- uid=410401301:136:1 -->

**Alice:** ...\*snicker\*

<!-- uid=410401301:137:1 -->

**Asuna:** Oh my gosh! Alice just laughed!!

<!-- uid=410401301:138:1 -->

**Alice:** I-I'm not laughing!  
It was just a gasp...!

<!-- uid=410401301:139:1 -->

**Alice:** N-Now come, we have important work

<!-- uid=410401301:140:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah!

<!-- uid=410401301:141:1 -->

**Reciter:** Every time a dog goes on a  
stroll...

<!-- uid=410401301:142:1 -->

**Player:** Got it!

<!-- uid=410401301:143:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ooh, nice!

<!-- uid=410401301:144:1 -->

**Asuna:** I think that's a karuta corner

<!-- uid=410401301:145:1 -->

**Reciter:** The days grow warmer, yet...

<!-- uid=410401301:146:1 -->

**Alice:** Wh-What speed!!

<!-- uid=410401301:147:1 -->

**Eugeo:** They're really fast...  
Their reaction speed might  
even beat Kirito's!

<!-- uid=410401301:148:1 -->

**Kirito:** That group over there is playing

<!-- uid=410401301:149:1 -->

**Asuna:** The second they hear the first  
phrase, they leap forward and  
snatch the right card...

<!-- uid=410401301:150:1 -->

**Asuna:** Each match is decided in the blink  
of an eye... These players are  
incredible!

<!-- uid=410401301:151:1 -->

**Alice:** I see what's going on.

<!-- uid=410401301:152:1 -->

**Alice:** I think their speed is

<!-- uid=410401301:153:1 -->

**Kirito:** Well, we're not just here to

join the tournament!

<!-- uid=410401301:154:1 -->

**Eugeo:** You did teach us how to play, but

<!-- uid=410401301:155:1 -->

**Kirito:** True... But this is a multi-day

<!-- uid=410401301:156:1 -->

**Kirito:** I know how smart you guys are;

have a lot of fun!

<!-- uid=410401301:157:1 -->

**Alice:** Fun? I knew it. You're just here

<!-- uid=410401301:158:1 -->

**Eugeo:** It was so obvious.

<!-- uid=410401301:159:1 -->

**Asuna:** Ahaha, yeah...

<!-- uid=410401301:160:1 -->

**Kirito:** H-Hey, is that a practice area?

**Kirito:** It looks like they'll teach you

<!-- uid=410401301:162:1 -->

**Alice:** \*sigh\* Fine. But I do not think

<!-- uid=410401301:163:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:164:1 -->

<!-- uid=410401301:165:1 -->

**Asuna:** Great! Let's get playing!

## Story 410401401 · Hearing your voice : Part 2

Event Story  
Eternal Bond - The Story of the Stars

**Reciter:** In the early dawn...

<!-- uid=410401401:1:1 -->

**Eugeo:** This one!

<!-- uid=410401401:2:1 -->

**Reciter:** When the mists on the Uji River  
slowly lift and clear...

<!-- uid=410401401:3:1 -->

**Alice:** Incorrect. This one.

<!-- uid=410401401:4:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Wait, really?  
I thought the next part was  
"as the moon comes into view"...?

<!-- uid=410401401:5:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ha ha, there are two poems that  
begin with "in the early dawn."

<!-- uid=410401401:6:1 -->

**Kirito:** In karuta, there are a number of  
sets of poems that start the same.  
You need to be careful.

<!-- uid=410401401:7:1 -->

**Alice:** Eugeo, we already went over this  
earlier. I expected better of you.

<!-- uid=410401401:8:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Y-Yes, ma'am...

<!-- uid=410401401:9:1 -->

**Asuna:** Hehe... Alice seems better than  
Eugeo when it comes to memorizing  
things.

<!-- uid=410401401:10:1 -->

**Kirito:** Yeah, she's really impressive.  
You and I played this when we were  
kids, so we remember a bunch...

<!-- uid=410401401:11:1 -->

**Kirito:** But the speed at which she's  
mastering every word and phrase  
is incredible.

<!-- uid=410401401:12:1 -->

**Alice:** Sweet words will earn you nothing  
but hard stares from me, Kirito.  
Now, quiet. The next card comes...

<!-- uid=410401401:13:1 -->

**Reciter:** If...

<!-- uid=410401401:14:1 -->

**Kirito:** This one!

<!-- uid=410401401:15:1 -->

**Alice:** Huh?!

<!-- uid=410401401:16:1 -->

**Reciter:** ...I see that bridge...

<!-- uid=410401401:17:1 -->

**Alice:** How did he know it would be "made  
white with a deep-laid frost"?!  
We only heard one syllable!

<!-- uid=410401401:18:1 -->

**Alice:** There are two poems that start  
with "if"...

<!-- uid=410401401:19:1 -->

**Asuna:** Good point. I think he rushed it  
and got lucky.

<!-- uid=410401401:20:1 -->

**Kirito:** Nope. No luck here! It was a  
simple trick someone just taught  
me.

<!-- uid=410401401:21:1 -->

**Kirito:** The intonation of the word is  
slightly different depending on  
what word follows.

<!-- uid=410401401:22:1 -->

**Kirito:** I'll admit it depends on the  
reciter, but if you know what  
to listen for, it's easy.

<!-- uid=410401401:23:1 -->

**Asuna:** W-Wow... Ha ha... "Easy," he says.

<!-- uid=410401401:24:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Yeah, there's no way I could pick

that...

<!-- uid=410401401:25:1 -->

**Alice:** Grr... This game is far more  
complex than I anticipated...

<!-- uid=410401401:26:1 -->

**Alice:** Very well! Teach me all these  
sly tricks and devilish secrets,  
Kirito!

<!-- uid=410401401:27:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ha ha, I'd be happy to.

<!-- uid=410401401:28:1 -->

**Kirito:** Then again...I'm a bit worried.

for us to sit here practicing...

<!-- uid=410401401:29:1 -->

**Asuna:** Why's that?

<!-- uid=410401401:30:1 -->

**Kirito:** Look, over there. There's a ton

tournament.

<!-- uid=410401401:31:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Wow, you're right.  
Look at all of them.

<!-- uid=410401401:32:1 -->

**Alice:** Even if all four of us win many

<!-- uid=410401401:34:1 -->

**Kirito:** but to play a lot of different

**Kirito:** and discover if our Hi-Mob is

**Kirito:** Great thinking! The more people we

<!-- uid=410401801:202:1 -->

**Alice:** You will soon forget us anyway...  
In which case, there should be no  
problem.

<!-- uid=410401801:203:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...!

<!-- uid=410401801:204:1 -->

**Alice:** I am sure you too have noticed how  
our memories have become hazy as  
of late...

<!-- uid=410401801:205:1 -->

**Alice:** It is only a matter of time before  
the same happens to all of you.

<!-- uid=410401801:206:1 -->

**Alice:** If you're going to forget us no  
matter what, there should be  
no reason to hesitate.

<!-- uid=410401801:207:1 -->

**Asuna:** I-I...

<!-- uid=410401801:208:1 -->

**Eugeo:** There you have it...  
Now, please. Hurry and kill us...

<!-- uid=410401801:209:1 -->

**Asuna:** We can't do that! And we're NOT  
going to forget you!

<!-- uid=410401801:210:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...

<!-- uid=410401801:211:1 -->

**Kirito:** You two are both terrible liars.  
Yes, we noticed that you were  
losing your memories.

<!-- uid=410401801:212:1 -->

**Kirito:** Or rather, that you were  
PRETENDING to lose them.

<!-- uid=410401801:213:1 -->

**Alice:** ...!

<!-- uid=410401801:214:1 -->

**Eugeo:** ...!

<!-- uid=410401801:215:1 -->

**Asuna:** Kirito...?

<!-- uid=410401801:216:1 -->

**Kirito:** It was all just an act so we  
wouldn't feel bad about killing  
you two, right?

<!-- uid=410401801:217:1 -->

**Kirito:** Ha ha, I've really got to hand it  
to you two.

<!-- uid=410401801:218:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Kirito...

<!-- uid=410401801:219:1 -->

**Kirito:** Eugeo... On the way here, you  
mixed up Sinon's name with  
Silica's. I'm guessing you were...

<!-- uid=410401801:220:1 -->

**Kirito:** ...trying to show how bad your  
memory had become,

<!-- uid=410401801:221:1 -->

**Kirito:** but I'm afraid it had  
the opposite effect.

<!-- uid=410401801:222:1 -->

**Kirito:** We couldn't have mentioned Silica  
more than a few times, but you  
remembered her name perfectly.

<!-- uid=410401801:223:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Ha ha... I guess I didn't think it  
through enough.

<!-- uid=410401801:224:1 -->

**Alice:** Eugeo!

<!-- uid=410401801:225:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Sorry, Alice... Looks like I'm  
done for anyway, whether  
they kill me or not...

<!-- uid=410401801:226:1 -->

**Eugeo:** I'm sorry I can't do the same  
for you...

<!-- uid=410401801:227:1 -->

**Alice:** ...No matter. I shall end my own  
life, with the honor and dignity  
befitting an Integrity Knight.

<!-- uid=410401801:228:1 -->

**Kirito:** Wait, wait! Don't give up yet!  
There HAS to be another way!

<!-- uid=410401801:229:1 -->

**Alice:** Then let us battle to the death.  
I shall come at you as if to kill  
you. You shall do the same.

<!-- uid=410401801:230:1 -->

**Kirito:** What? No, I'm not going to do  
that! I'm not giving up!

<!-- uid=410401801:231:1 -->

**Alice:** Please, Kirito. We can't bear to  
see you all so sad... to see this  
world fall apart because of us!

<!-- uid=410401801:232:1 -->

**Alice:** You must understand! This is  
the only solution left to us! We  
simply don't have any more time!

<!-- uid=410401801:233:1 -->

**Asuna:** No... It's too awful!  
Things can't end like this...

<!-- uid=410401801:234:1 -->

**Kirito:** Damn it...!

<!-- uid=410401801:235:1 -->

**Yuuki:** Isn't there anything we can do?

<!-- uid=410401801:236:1 -->

**Sinon:** I feel totally powerless...

<!-- uid=410401801:237:1 -->

**Leafa:** If only we knew how to get them  
back home...

<!-- uid=410401801:238:1 -->

**Urdr:** ...

<!-- uid=410401801:239:1 -->

**Urdr:** Fairy warriors and foreign  
elements who lead this world  
to destruction...

<!-- uid=410401801:240:1 -->

**Urdr:** The world has learned much from  
your journey and has continued to  
search for new power and methods.

<!-- uid=410401801:241:1 -->

**Urdr:** Now it has learned of something  
else. Foreign elements like you  
were once considered anathema.

<!-- uid=410401801:242:1 -->

**Urdr:** But, with enough time, it seems  
that even you can be accommodated.

<!-- uid=410401801:243:1 -->

**Kirito:** Wh-What are you saying?

<!-- uid=410401801:244:1 -->

**Urdr:** Through the connection you have  
forged with the fairies, the world  
has managed to trace...

<!-- uid=410401801:245:1 -->

**Urdr:** ...the path of quantum circuits  
you used to come here and to  
find your original time stream.

<!-- uid=410401801:246:1 -->

**Kirito:** What?! Then, you mean... you've  
found a way for them to go home?!

<!-- uid=410401801:247:1 -->

**Leafa:** Um, Urdr... I'm not sure that part  
about quantum circuits actually  
makes sense...

<!-- uid=410401801:248:1 -->

**Sinon:** Don't ruin the moment, Leafa. I'm  
pretty sure she's speaking on  
behalf of Cardinal now.

<!-- uid=410401801:249:1 -->

**Urdr:** This would not have happened  
without you and the fairies  
reaching an understanding...

<!-- uid=410401801:250:1 -->

**Kirito:** So then... during the defrag,  
Cardinal came to understand  
Alice and Eugeo as people?

<!-- uid=410401801:251:1 -->

**Asuna:** And that must have helped it find  
a way back to their world!

<!-- uid=410401801:252:1 -->

**Eugeo:** So... does this mean we can go  
home?

<!-- uid=410401801:253:1 -->

**Alice:** Can we really trust you, Urdr?

<!-- uid=410401801:254:1 -->

**Urdr:** Yes, foreign elements... Now that  
the world understands you, you  
are no longer foreign.

<!-- uid=410401801:255:1 -->

**Urdr:** Now, come. There's no time to  
waste. Both of you, take my hands.

<!-- uid=410401801:256:1 -->

**Kirito:** Eugeo, Alice...

<!-- uid=410401801:257:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Kirito... Everyone... Thank you.  
I guess this is goodbye...

<!-- uid=410401801:258:1 -->

**Kirito:** I guess so... Oh, here, take this  
Divine Object with you. It should  
help you recover.

<!-- uid=410401801:259:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Ha ha, good idea. Okay, I'll take  
Fenrir's Divine Object with me.

<!-- uid=410401801:260:1 -->

**Alice:** Thank you for all your help.  
I know that we said we would  
never forget one another...

<!-- uid=410401801:261:1 -->

**Alice:** But there's no telling what may  
happen once we return to our  
own world.

<!-- uid=410401801:262:1 -->

**Alice:** However... even if we do happen  
to forget you, I swear to you  
now...

<!-- uid=410401801:263:1 -->

**Alice:** Should we see each other again,  
I shall be honored to greet you  
as comrades in arms.

<!-- uid=410401801:264:1 -->

**Asuna:** Alice... The same goes for me too!

<!-- uid=410401801:265:1 -->

**Kirito:** Absolutely. This isn't  
farewell forever.

<!-- uid=410401801:266:1 -->

**Kirito:** I'm sure that someday  
we'll see each other again...  
Take care, you two.

<!-- uid=410401801:267:1 -->

**Eugeo:** Thank you, Kirito, Asuna...  
All of you... Thank you!

<!-- uid=410401801:268:1 -->

**Alice:** I promise that someday,  
when our worlds cross paths...  
We shall meet again.

<!-- uid=410401801:269:1 -->

**Kirito:** You bet!

<!-- uid=410401801:270:1 -->
